### [1] [Boundary-contact repolarization provides a mechanism for directional guidance of neural crest cell collectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12721)
*Aditya Shankar Paspunurwar,Hector Gomez*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过计算模型揭示：神经嵴细胞的集体定向迁移不仅依赖空间限制，还需边界接触诱导的极性重定向与自生成化学信号协同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经嵴细胞（NCCs）是研究胚胎发育中集体迁移及类癌症侵袭行为的经典体系。已有研究确立了共吸引、运动接触抑制、极性持续性和组织包围在引导NCC迁移中的作用，但尚不清楚在受限环境中NCC迁移是仅由空间限制造成，还是也包含由受限边界接触诱导的极性重定向反应。

Method: 构建了一个计算模型，将自生成化学信号的输运与接触介导的极性重定向及机械相互作用相耦合，用以模拟受限环境下的NCC集体迁移。

Result: 模型显示，细胞-边界接触可重新定向细胞极性并在受限条件下维持定向迁移；若无受限则不存在持久的集体方向性。此外，在保留机械相互作用的同时抑制非允许性受限边界介导的极性重定向，会阻止持久的定向迁移，表明单纯的空间限制在模型中并不充分。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了自生成化学信号与受限组织接触共同调控NCC集体导向的可实验验证机制，为研究发育性迁移缺陷及调控集体组织侵袭的潜在策略提供了基础。

Abstract: Neural crest cells (NCCs) provide a well-established system for investigating collective migration during embryonic development and exhibit migratory behaviors with parallels to cancer invasion. Previous studies establish roles for co-attraction, contact inhibition of locomotion, polarity persistence, and tissue confinement in guiding NCC migration. However, it remains unclear whether NCC migration in confined environments occurs through spatial restriction alone or also through a repolarization response induced by contact with boundaries of confinement. By developing a computational model that couples self-generated chemical transport with contact-mediated repolarization and mechanical interactions, we show that cell-boundary contact can reorient cell polarity and sustain directional migration under confinement, whereas no persistent collective direction emerges without confinement. Moreover, suppressing contact-mediated repolarization from non-permissive confinement boundaries while preserving the mechanical interactions prevents sustained directional migration, suggesting that spatial restriction alone is insufficient in the model. Together, these findings establish an experimentally testable mechanism through which self-generated chemical signals and contact with confining tissues jointly regulate collective guidance of NCCs. These insights provide a basis for investigating developmental migration defects and potential strategies to regulate collective tissue invasion.

</details>


### [2] [Efficient Transport Networks through Edge Pruning by Relative Resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13582)
*Mathieu Le Verge-Serandour,Viktor Vasilev,Lara Szeimies,Karen Alim*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖局部规则、无需全局目标与特定边界条件的输运网络自适应方法：通过按相对阻力依次剪枝，移除阻力高于其余网络绕行路径的边，从而得到稀疏、富含环路且低耗散的网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输运网络在生物与工程系统中至关重要，但如何在缺乏全局目标函数和特定边界条件的情况下，仅凭局部规则实现高效自适应，仍是一个核心问题。

Method: 受生物输运网络观测启发，采用基于相对阻力的迭代剪枝策略：对每条边，比较其自身阻力与经由网络其余部分路由该流量所需的阻力，若前者更高则将其删除。该冗余度量完全由拓扑决定，局部且不依赖边界条件。

Result: 通过这种局部、无边界条件的冗余度量进行自适应，能够得到稀疏、环路丰富且低耗散的输运网络。

Conclusion: 结果表明，高效输运网络的自适应未必需要全局优化或特定边界条件，仅靠拓扑定义的局部剪枝规则即可涌现出稀疏、多环、低耗散的优良网络结构，为理解生物网络及设计工程网络提供了新思路。

Abstract: Transport networks are key to living and engineered systems. How can transport networks adapt efficiently using only local rules, without a global objective or specific boundary conditions? Inspired by observations in biological transport networks, we adapt networks by subsequently pruning edges by relative resistance, thus removing edges whose resistance is higher than the resistance for routing flow through the rest of the network. We find that adaptation by such a local, boundary-condition-free measure of redundancy, set by topology alone, yields sparse, loop-rich, low-dissipation networks.

</details>

### [3] [Neuromorphic Satellite Intelligence: A Formal Framework for Event-Driven Architectures, Spike-Based Algorithms, and Quantitative Energy-Accuracy Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12471)
*Suresh Kumar TP,Vikas Agnihotri*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究卫星星载计算中脉冲神经网络（SNN）应用，构建能耗形式化模型，提出三种原创算法，建立能效-精度-时延（EAL）权衡面与抗辐射架构分类，并给出多任务案例量化设计点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星载计算面临根本性矛盾：任务智能需求单调增长，而功率、质量与抗辐射预算固定不变。传统GPU/FPGA方案在此约束下能效受限，需探索类脑脉冲计算范式以突破瓶颈。

Method: (1) 建立卫星工作负载的脉冲神经计算形式化数学模型，推导能耗关于网络稀疏度、放电率与突触扇出的闭式表达式；(2) 提出三种原创算法：用于姿态估计的脉冲卡尔曼-互补滤波器（SKCF）、用于航天器健康监测的时间对比异常检测器（TCAD）、用于认知卫星链路的脉冲自适应调制编码（SAMC），均附伪代码与复杂度解析界；(3) 构建定量EAL权衡面，基于Loihi与SpiNNaker的已发表硅测量数据进行数值填充，并与FPGA、GPU基线对比；(4) 完善四类抗辐射神经形态架构分类及故障率模型；(5) 以受约束STDP规则形式化在轨持续学习，并证明其权重漂移有界。

Result: 针对12U立方星对地观测、GEO通信载荷与自主深空探测器三类案例给出量化设计点；分析表明，在代表性卫星工作负载下，神经形态实现相比GPU基线可实现8至47倍能耗降低，同时推理精度损失控制在2.1%以内。

Conclusion: 脉冲神经计算可在固定功率与辐射预算下显著提升星载计算能效，且精度损失可接受；EAL权衡面、抗辐射架构分类与有界STDP学习规则共同构成卫星神经形态计算的可量化设计框架。

Abstract: Satellite onboard computing faces an irreducible tension: mission intelligence requirements grow monotonically while power, mass, and radiation budgets remain fixed. This paper advances the state of the art on three fronts. First, we develop a formal mathematical model of spiking neural computation for satellite workloads, deriving closed-form expressions for energy consumption as a function of network sparsity, firing rate, and synaptic fan-out. Second, we propose three original algorithms -- a Spiking Kalman-Complementary Filter (SKCF) for attitude estimation, a Temporal Contrast Anomaly Detector (TCAD) for spacecraft health monitoring, and a Spike-Based Adaptive Modulation and Coding (SAMC) scheme for cognitive satellite links -- each with pseudocode and analytical complexity bounds. Third, we introduce a quantitative Energy-Accuracy-Latency (EAL) trade-off surface and populate it with numerical estimates derived from published silicon measurements of Loihi and SpiNNaker, enabling direct comparison with FPGA and GPU baselines. A taxonomy of four radiation-aware neuromorphic architecture classes is refined with fault-rate models. Continual on-orbit learning is formalized through a constrained Spike-Timing Dependent Plasticity (STDP) rule that provably bounds weight drift. Case studies for a 12U CubeSat Earth observation mission, a GEO communications payload, and an autonomous deep-space probe provide concrete, quantified design points. Our analysis shows that neuromorphic implementations can achieve 8 to 47 times energy reduction over GPU baselines while maintaining inference accuracy within 2.1\% for representative satellite workloads.

</details>


### [4] [Energy-Efficient Spiking Neural Networks for Sensing-Aided Beam Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12507)
*Nhan Thanh Nguyen,Phuong Nam Tran,Mengyuan Ma,Huy T. Nguyen,Ahmed Alkhateeb,Markku Juntti*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文将脉冲神经网络（SNN）应用于感知辅助毫米波波束预测，系统比较SNN与ANN在位置、视觉及多模态输入下的表现，发现视觉/多模态场景中SNN在同等能耗下精度更高、在同等精度下能耗低至一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知辅助波束预测可利用视觉、位置等互补模态信息降低毫米波系统的波束训练开销，但由此产生的感知与推理流水线能耗较高，因此需要引入高能效的脉冲神经网络（SNN）来替代传统人工神经网络（ANN）。

Method: 作者构建了面向波束预测的SNN架构，并在仅位置、仅视觉以及多模态三种输入条件下，与ANN在精度和能耗两个维度上进行系统性对比；同时还分析了SNN精度受限的来源。

Result: 在仅位置感知下，紧凑型ANN始终优于SNN；而在视觉和多模态感知下，SNN在相似能耗下精度高于ANN，在相当精度下能耗最多降低一个数量级。例如多模态SNN取得58.08%的top-1精度，比能耗最接近的ANN高出18.11个百分点。此外，SNN的精度瓶颈来自层间的二值通信而非模型规模。

Conclusion: SNN在能效敏感的感知辅助波束预测中具有应用潜力，但受层间二值通信限制，其精度仍存在固有局限。

Abstract: Sensing-aided beam prediction can reduce the beam-training overhead of millimeter-wave (mmWave) systems by leveraging environmental information from complementary modalities such as vision and position. However, the resulting sensing and inference pipeline can be energy-intensive, motivating the use of energy-efficient spiking neural networks (SNNs) as an alternative to conventional artificial neural networks (ANNs). In this work, we develop SNN architectures for beam prediction and systematically compare them with ANNs using position-only, vision-only, and multimodal inputs. Our results show that compact ANNs consistently outperform SNNs for position-only sensing. In contrast, for vision and multimodal sensing, SNNs achieve higher accuracy than ANNs at similar energy costs and require up to an order of magnitude less energy at a comparable accuracy. For example, the multimodal SNN achieves 58.08% top-1 accuracy, outperforming the closest-energy ANN by 18.11 percentage points. We further show that SNN accuracy is limited by the binary communication between layers rather than by model size. These results demonstrate the potential of SNNs for energy-efficient sensing-aided beam prediction while highlighting their accuracy limitation.

</details>


### [5] [REMBGO for Evaluation-Limited Optimization: CEC2022 Benchmarking and EAF-STATCOM Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13471)
*Vikram Chekuri,Narapareddy Ramarao,Prashanth N A*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出REMBGO——EMBGO在受限函数评估预算下的精炼版本，强调其贡献在于种群保留、尺度归一化探索与恢复机制的集成而非单一算子，并在CEC2022及EAF–STATCOM控制器调参中验证，主张其收益必须与评估预算共同陈述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元启发式算法比较常在远超昂贵工程仿真可承受的评估预算下进行，导致结论在受限预算场景中缺乏适用性。

Method: REMBGO集成种群保留、尺度归一化探索与恢复机制；在CEC2022以1040次函数评估为主预算、并辅以预算阶梯作描述性分析；开展组件移除研究（含多重性校正）；应用于EAF–STATCOM控制器调参，冻结增益向量后无重调迁移至20个预设随机弧实现（210次固定增益仿真）。

Result: 组件移除研究显示无单一机制经受住多重性校正；固定增益下210次仿真全部可行；增益向量主效应仅占交叉验证变异的3.6%–5.8%，交互与残差约61.4%；REMBGO–EMBGO配对差异符号依控制器而异且不显著。

Conclusion: REMBGO应被视为资源感知型精炼算法，其收益须与评估预算一并说明，而非普遍优越的优化器。

Abstract: Metaheuristic comparisons are often reported at evaluation budgets far larger than an expensive engineering simulation can afford. This paper presents REMBGO, an evaluation-budget-aware refinement of EMBGO. Its contribution is not any single operator: it is the integration of population preservation, scale-normalized exploration and recovery mechanisms whose parent-relative benefit is concentrated under restricted function-evaluation budgets. REMBGO is evaluated on CEC2022 at a primary, application-aligned budget of 1040 function evaluations, with a surrounding budget ladder reported descriptively to characterise budget dependence rather than as a family of controlled hypotheses. A component-removal study finds no individual mechanism whose removal survives multiplicity correction, consistent with an integrated rather than a single-operator contribution. The algorithm is then applied to EAF--STATCOM controller tuning, and the resulting frozen gain vectors are transferred without retuning to twenty pre-specified unseen stochastic arc realizations ($210$ fixed-gain simulations, all feasible). The selected gain-vector main effect accounts for only $3.6$--$5.8\%$ of the crossed validation variation, against approximately $61.4\%$ for interaction plus residual, and the matched REMBGO--EMBGO paired differences are controller-dependent in sign and not statistically significant. REMBGO is therefore presented as a resource-aware refinement whose benefit must be stated together with the evaluation budget, not as a universally superior optimizer.

</details>


### [6] [DyssomniaNN: Multi-Scale Spiking Neural Modeling of Human Sleep Disorders and Perceived Restedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13918)
*Daniel Szelogowski*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文提出DyssomniaNN，一个多尺度生物物理计算框架，将睡眠稳态与昼夜节律的双过程模型、下丘脑交互flip-flop回路以及循环性皮质丘脑LIF脉冲神经网络耦合，用于模拟五类临床睡眠队列的72小时光周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床睡眠医学中普遍存在客观睡眠时长与主观日间功能损害之间的显著分离，如矛盾性失眠中的睡眠状态误知觉和1型发作性睡病中的边界不稳定性。现有研究缺乏能够跨尺度整合睡眠调节机制并复现这些临床分离现象的机制性计算平台。

Method: 构建DyssomniaNN多尺度生物物理框架：耦合Borbély睡眠稳态双过程与昼夜节律驱动、Saper下丘脑交互flip-flop回路，以及循环性皮质丘脑泄漏积分发放（LIF）脉冲神经网络。在统一72小时光周期下形式化五个队列：健康对照、昼夜节律睡眠-觉醒障碍（相位延迟型）、慢性失眠、矛盾性失眠和1型发作性睡病。合成局部场电位与PhysioNet Sleep-EDF数据库的经验人类脑电频谱对比验证，并提出Well-Rested Index量化恢复效能。

Result: 合成局部场电位在所有经典频段与经验脑电频谱高度吻合（频谱平均绝对误差0.33–0.35）。仿真显示睡眠时长无法预测恢复性修复：矛盾性失眠总睡眠时间正常（21.15 h）但恢复率低（37.6%），源于未受抑制的皮质beta频段侵入；发作性睡病的下丘脑分泌素缺乏导致严重夜间碎片化（入睡后清醒781 min）和35次睡眠起始快速眼动期。

Conclusion: DyssomniaNN为机制性睡眠研究和虚拟神经药理学提供了一个in silico平台，能够统一复现跨尺度睡眠调节机制与临床分离现象。

Abstract: Clinical sleep medicine repeatedly documents profound divergences between objective sleep duration and subjective daytime impairment, notably exemplified by sleep state misperception in paradoxical insomnia and boundary instability in narcolepsy type 1. Here, we present DyssomniaNN: a multi-scale biophysical computational framework coupling Borbély's two-process regulation of sleep homeostasis and circadian drive with Saper's hypothalamic reciprocal flip-flop circuit and a recurrent corticothalamic leaky integrate-and-fire spiking neural network. We formalize five clinical cohorts across an identical 72-hour photoperiod: healthy control, circadian rhythm sleep-wake disorder (delayed-phase), chronic insomnia, paradoxical insomnia, and narcolepsy type 1. The synthetic local field potential converges closely with empirical human electroencephalography spectra from the PhysioNet Sleep-EDF database across all canonical frequency bands (spectral mean absolute error of 0.33--0.35). Furthermore, we introduce the Well-Rested Index to quantify restorative efficacy. Simulations reveal that sleep duration fails to predict restorative recovery: paradoxical insomnia achieves normal total sleep time (21.15 h) yet yields low restoration (37.6%) from unsuppressed cortical beta-band intrusion, whereas hypocretin deficiency in narcolepsy produces severe nocturnal fragmentation (781 min of wake after sleep onset) and 35 sleep-onset rapid eye movement periods. DyssomniaNN provides an in silico platform for mechanistic sleep research and virtual neuropharmacology.

</details>

### [7] [FlyHack: Stress-Testing Mechanistic Claims in a Connectome-Constrained Visual Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13018)
*Aashish Dhawan*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文以果蝇运动视觉模型 flyvis 为例，系统压力测试了连接组约束网络的机制结论对比较模型、干预方式、回路表征与训练解的依赖，主张此类模型的机制性主张必须限定在特定模型家族、干预、表征与训练解范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接组约束神经网络虽使生物组件可直接干预，但其机制结论仍可能依赖于所选的比较模型、干预手段、回路表征以及训练得到的解，因此需要系统性检验这些依赖的稳健性。

Method: 在 flyvis 果蝇运动视觉模型中开展压力测试：使用 50 个模型组成的集成、任务匹配的因果 CNN 对照，并重复干预实验；在行为层面考察三级 reverse-phi 反转、对抗方向反转及参数匹配 CNN 对比；在机制层面比较一阶边缘归因 patch 与零对齐得分；并评估贪心细胞类型集合、T4/T5 输出沉默、连接集剪枝及双侧响应幅度校验。

Result: 行为上，三级 reverse-phi 反转强烈依赖周期与速度并同样出现于任务匹配 CNN，两族均未复现静止锯齿响应；对抗方向反转在 flyvis 中需 0.21–0.27 倍刺激对比度扰动，CNN 为 0.13–0.25，但更大 CNN 上差距基本消失。机制上，留出均值替换时一阶边缘归因 patch 的 pooled AUROC 0.970、Spearman 0.872，而零对齐得分仅 0.933 与 0.126。回路结论随表征与集成成员变化：50 个模型中 25 个满足 T4/T5 调谐判据，T4/T5 输出沉默在 8 个片段中最多只影响 2 个；6 个首次成功模型中连接集可剪枝至 13–31 个新增类，附加双侧幅度校验后仅 2 个仍达调谐阈值。

Conclusion: 连接组约束模型使机制假设可直接检验，但其所得结论必须谨慎限定于具体的模型家族、干预方式、回路表征与训练解，需采用更窄的适用范围来解读。

Abstract: Connectome-constrained neural networks make biological components directly accessible to intervention, but their mechanistic conclusions can still depend on the comparison model, intervention, circuit representation and trained solution. We stress-test these dependencies in flyvis, a \emph{Drosophila} motion-vision model, using its 50-model ensemble, task-matched causal CNN controls and replicated interventions. At the level of model behaviour, three-level reverse-phi reversal in flyvis is strongly period- and velocity-dependent and also appears in task-matched CNNs; neither family reproduces the fly's stationary-sawtooth response. Adversarial direction reversal requires a perturbation bound of 0.21--0.27 times stimulus contrast in flyvis versus 0.13--0.25 in CNNs, but the gap over a parameter-matched CNN largely closes for larger CNNs. At the level of mechanism, matching attribution to the intervention matters: for held-out-mean replacement, first-order edge attribution patching reaches pooled AUROC 0.970 and Spearman 0.872, versus 0.933 and 0.126 when its zero-aligned score is evaluated against the same ground truth. Circuit conclusions also vary with representation and ensemble member. Greedy cell-type sets meet a model-relative T4/T5 tuning criterion in 25 of 50 models, while T4/T5 output silencing reduces the tested optic-flow behaviour to at most two of eight clips in 40. In six models where the cell-type search first succeeds, the corresponding connection sets can be pruned to 13--31 added classes; under an additional two-sided response-amplitude check, only two of those six first-success cell-type sets still meet the tuning threshold. These results support a narrower use of connectome-constrained models: they make mechanistic hypotheses directly testable, but the resulting claims must be scoped to the model family, intervention, representation and trained solution.

</details>


### [8] [Excitability across scales: a unifying EI model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13275)
*Thomas S. J. Burger,Rodolphe Sepulchre*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 提出一种时空兴奋-抑制（EI）模型，用共同混合反馈架构统一神经元兴奋性的细胞尺度与群体尺度建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞尺度与群体尺度常用不同原理建模神经元兴奋性，难以研究微观机制如何塑造宏观活动，需要跨尺度统一的框架。

Method: 构建时空EI模型：保留Hodgkin-Huxley模型的电导型回路结构，用忆导（memductance）描述本征与突触电流，其时空电压依赖性编码相应尺度的动力学；最小形式中时间兴奋性来自快速兴奋反馈被慢速抑制反馈对抗，空间兴奋性来自短程兴奋被长程抑制对抗，复现Amari神经场的侧抑制motif。

Abstract: Neuronal excitability is commonly modeled using different principles at the cellular and population scales, making it difficult to study how microscopic mechanisms shape macroscopic activity. We introduce a spatiotemporal excitatory-inhibitory (EI) model that unifies these scales through a common mixed-feedback architecture. The model retains the conductance-based circuit structure of Hodgkin-Huxley models and describes intrinsic and synaptic currents through memductances whose temporal and spatial voltage dependence encodes dynamics at the relevant scale. In its minimal form, temporal excitability arises from fast excitatory feedback opposed by slower inhibitory feedback, while spatial excitability arises from short-range excitation opposed by longer-range inhibition, recovering the core lateral-inhibition motif of Amari neural fields. We show that combining these mechanisms gives rise to population excitability in strongly heterogeneous populations. In particular, the model generates population spikes that are localized in both space and time and possess population-level scales distinct from those of individual neuronal spikes, despite substantial cell-to-cell parameter variability. We then show that both intrinsic temporal excitability and spatial synaptic feedback are required for coherent mean-field events and robust spatiotemporal population rhythms. Finally, selective modulation of memductances in a subset of the population tunes the frequency of the population rhythm, illustrating how changes at the cellular scale can shape macroscopic dynamics. By separating circuit architecture from modeling scale, the proposed framework provides a physically interpretable approach to analyzing and designing neuronal excitability across scales.

</details>

Result: ARC明确了成人依恋反馈调节的时序假设，并提出可比较的差异建模约束与调节/结果变量的层级定位，使该框架具备可操作化与可证伪性。

Conclusion: ARC将人格层面依恋维度与逐时反馈调节机制连接起来，为成人浪漫关系中的依恋调节研究提供了整合、简约且可实证检验的理论框架；其核心贡献在于时序明确化、差异分数的建模约束检验及调节变量的分层界定。

Abstract: Adult attachment is commonly represented by attachment anxiety and avoidance, but these person-level dimensions do not by themselves specify how momentary relational conditions are perceived, compared with desired conditions, translated into action, and updated after interpersonal feedback. In ARC, control refers to feedback-based regulation toward relational goals in the control-theoretic sense, not interpersonal dominance, coercion, or a generalized need for control. ARC does not claim a novel control-system architecture. Instead, it synthesizes established control-theoretic attachment ideas, secure-base representations, perceived partner responsiveness, relational discrepancy, dyadic regulation, and recovery into a parsimonious sequence for adult romantic relationships: perceived relational state, desired relational state, discrepancy, regulatory action, partner feedback, state updating, and recovery. The framework treats the discrepancy score itself as a falsifiable modeling restriction by comparing it with perceived-state-only, unconstrained perceived-plus-desired-state, and asymmetric discrepancy alternatives. Attachment anxiety and avoidance form core regulation moderators. Secure-base representation is a candidate requiring incremental prediction of recovery outcomes. Mentalized affectivity and regulation flexibility remain optional moderators, while adult disorganization is an independent extension outcome.

Abstract: Developmental dyslexia (DLX) affects 5-12% of the global population and impairs learning to read. We investigated whether brain entrainment to non-speech rhythms could predict DLX in 48 participants listening to white noise amplitude-modulated at rates matching Spanish speech units. Phase-amplitude coupling (PAC) analysis of electroencephalography (EEG) signals yielded accurate DLX prediction for the 40 Hz stimulus using the Heights Ratio PAC measure (balanced accuracy 0.929, p = 0.0069, in the best case), indicating a stronger phonemic entrainment deficit in dyslexic individuals. Regression analysis in control (CTL) participants showed a strong dependence between reading performance scores and greater entrainment in language-associated brain regions. In particular, participants with poorer phonemic awareness, reading comprehension, or rapid automatized naming showed greater right-hemisphere entrainment to the 40 Hz stimulus. This finding adds evidence for the hemispheric compensatory mechanisms hypothesized in the literature. In conclusion, our methodology identifies dyslexia-specific entrainment patterns near language-associated brain regions, together with compensatory entrainment in right-hemisphere areas.

Abstract: The mammalian neocortex is a complex structure with many interacting regions and cell types. Multiple efforts have recently generated large datasets from multiple cortical regions to determine generalizing principles of cortical organization and activity. One such generalizing principle is the concept of a cortical hierarchy - low-dimensional macroscopic gradients of biological properties across cortical areas and a hierarchy of timescales for information processing. Such gradients in properties along a cortical hierarchy may explain how different cortical regions serve different computational roles (i.e. sensory processing versus working memory). We set out to investigate such principles using biologically realistic simulations. Starting from our previously published model of the mouse primary visual cortex (VISp) with updated connectivity reflecting recent large-scale connectomics and synaptic physiology datasets, we generated multiple versions of the cortical circuit model with increased connectivity as measured along the cortical hierarchy. We then measure the resulting neural activity in response to a simulated single-cell photostimulation paradigm and thalamocortical inputs. We found that overall circuit stability increased across the cortical hierarchy, due to both increases in connection density and increases in the relative proportion of SST cells. The results of this study link cortical cell types to population-level activity, generating insight into the function of local circuits across cortex.

These early maturation defects persisted during disease progression and preceded the reduction in spine density observed at P11. For example, the transcription factor Meis2 is selectively expressed in the GABAergic subclass of ACs. In this study, we focused on how Meis2 regulates the development of GABAergic ACs. The deletion of Meis2 led to a ~60% reduction in total GABAergic ACs in the retina. This loss of GABAergic ACs occurred only after postnatal day 5 (P5), suggesting Meis2 is not required for early specification but may be required for survival. To further investigate cell death and survival, we quantified cleaved caspase-3 positive cells in the retina at several developmental timepoints. In Meis2-CKO mice, we found an increase in cleaved caspase-3 immunolabeling at P5 and P6 timepoints coinciding with the reduction of GABAergic ACs. Further analysis of GABAergic AC subtypes demonstrated a subtype-specific cell death in Meis2-CKO retinas. For example, the total number of dopaminergic (TH) ACs were significantly reduced, whereas starburst (ChAT) ACs remained unchanged. These findings suggest that Meis2 is important for the survival of a subset of GABAergic AC subtypes in the mammalian retina.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: vSub是海马结构的重要输出节点，参与成瘾、焦虑等精神健康相关神经环路；兴奋-抑制失衡及GABA能信号改变是多种神经系统疾病的共同特征。既往研究多聚焦单一interneuron亚型，而整体局部GABA能神经元群激活的网络后果尚不清楚，因此需要明确广泛GABA能群体被募集时的功能效应。

Method: 采用mDlx依赖策略在腹侧海马GABA能神经元中表达channelrhodopsin，在麻醉大鼠vSub进行in vivo电生理记录；以20 Hz（行为光遗传研究中常用频率）重复激活GABA能神经元群，分析锥体神经元放电及刺激后网络活动；结合离体电生理实验评估网络依赖效应与短时程突触可塑性。

Result: 20 Hz重复激活GABA能群体在刺激期间稳健抑制锥体神经元放电；刺激后出现网络依赖的活动增强，其幅度随基线活动、性别和试次变化。离体实验支持网络依赖效应，并显示随重复刺激试次演变的短时程突触可塑性，提示突触前和突触后机制共同参与。

Conclusion: 募集更广泛的GABA能神经元群不仅产生直接抑制，还可引发依赖刺激历史和环路状态的动态网络反应。解读GABA能环路操纵的功能后果时，需重视刺激频率、时间模式和网络背景。

Abstract: The ventral subiculum (vSub), a major output node of the hippocampal formation, has been implicated in diverse neurological disorders. Disruption of excitatory-inhibitory balance is a common feature of neurological disorders, with altered GABAergic signaling representing a critical component. More recently, the vSub has gained increasing attention as a component of neural circuits underlying emotional and motivational regulation, through its extensive connections with regions implicated in mental health disorders such as addiction and anxiety. Our previous work in models of alcohol use disorder and comorbid anxiety identified an important role for GABAergic inhibition in regulating vSub excitability, prompting us to investigate the functional consequences of recruiting the broader local GABAergic population. Although previous studies have examined individual interneuron subtypes, the network consequences of activating the global population of local GABAergic neurons remain poorly understood. To address this question, we expressed channelrhodopsin using an mDlx-dependent strategy to target GABAergic neurons in the ventral hippocampus and performed in vivo electrophysiological recordings from vSub neurons in anesthetized rats. Repeated activation of this GABAergic population at 20 Hz, a stimulation frequency commonly used in behavioral optogenetic studies, produced robust suppression of pyramidal neuron firing during stimulation. This direct inhibition was accompanied by network-dependent increases in post-stimulus activity that varied as a function of baseline activity, sex, and trial number. Complementary ex vivo electrophysiological experiments further supported network-dependent effects and revealed short-term synaptic plasticity that evolved across repeated stimulation trials, reflecting contributions from both pre- and postsynaptic mechanisms. Together, these findings demonstrate that recruitment of the broader GABAergic population produces effects extending beyond direct inhibition and can engage dynamic network responses that depend on stimulation history and circuit state. These findings highlight the importance of stimulation frequency, timing, and network context when interpreting the functional consequences of GABAergic circuit manipulation.

</details>


### [16] [Newborns modulate and synchronise their Non-Nutritive Sucking behaviour to the temporal structure of sound](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.06.756750v1)
*Arioli, M.,Federici, A.,Silvestri, V.,Santini, M.,Colombo, L.,Gianni, M. L.,Bottari, D.,Macchi Cassia, V.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究通过记录41名健康足月新生儿（出生24–72小时）的非营养性吸吮（NNS），考察其能否将吸吮行为与听觉刺激的时间结构同步。结果显示新生儿可选择性对齐刺激特异性的时间规律，表明感觉运动同步的早期基础在出生后数小时内即已存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感觉运动同步（将运动行为与外部刺激时间结构对齐的能力）被认为在发育过程中逐渐形成，但其起源尚不明确。作者希望探究新生儿在出生最初几天内是否已具备将吸吮行为适应并对齐听觉刺激时间结构的能力。

Method: 41名健康足月新生儿（24–72小时龄）接受两种各三分钟的听觉流：一种具有稳定时间模式的高结构化流，一种时间变异性较大的高变异流，同时连续记录其非营养性吸吮（NNS）。分析NNS频率与压力的变化，并采用幅度平方相干（magnitude-squared coherence）在各听觉流特异的频率上量化NNS与声音的耦合。

Result: 结构化刺激促进了更有组织的NNS，吸吮爆发多于变异流，并提高了吸吮力度——第一分钟内吸吮峰值率相对变异流出现暂时性升高，且吸吮压力相对静默条件更大。相干分析显示，无论结构化还是高变异刺激，相干性均在各听觉流特异频率上选择性升高。

Conclusion: 新生儿在出生后数小时内，其NNS行为即可选择性地与刺激特异性的时间规律对齐，即使这些规律可预测性较弱且缺乏主导周期性。这种早期提取时间结构并协调运动行为的能力，可能为后续感觉运动同步的发展奠定基础。

Abstract: Sensorimotor synchronization, the ability to align motor behaviour with the temporal structure of external stimuli, is thought to emerge gradually during development, but its origins remain unclear. Here, we investigated whether newborns can adapt their sucking behaviour and synchronise it to the temporal structure of auditory stimuli within the first days of life. Forty-one healthy full-term newborns (24-72 hours old) were exposed to two three-minute auditory streams differing in temporal structure: a highly structured stream with a stable temporal pattern and a highly variant stream with greater temporal variability, while their non-nutritive sucking (NNS) was continuously recorded. We assessed changes in NNS frequency and pressure and quantified NNS-sound coupling using magnitude-squared coherence at frequencies distinctive to each auditory stream. Structured stimulation promoted a more organised NNS, with more suck bursts than the variant stream, and increased sucking vigour, with a transiently higher suck peak rate during the first minute relative to the variant stream and greater suck pressure relative to silence. Coherence analyses revealed that coherence was selectively higher at the frequencies distinctive to each auditory stream, both for the structured and the highly variant stimulus. These findings show that, within hours of birth, NNS behaviour can selectively align with stimulus-specific temporal regularities, even when these are weakly predictable and lack a dominant periodicity. This early capacity to extract temporal structure and coordinate motor behaviour may provide a foundation for the subsequent development of sensorimotor synchronisation.

</details>


### [17] [Global statistical constraint and local partner-sensitive inference support dynamic, open-ended verbal coordination](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.03.756413v1)
*Zhang, T.,Shaw, D. J.,Zhou, S.,Zhu, L.,Lu, C.*

TL;DR: 结合深度语言模型与认知计算建模，研究发现开放式言语交流中的词汇对齐源于"全局语境约束"与"局部伙伴敏感调整"的分工：个体全局语义搜索空间收窄、局部不确定性上升，而伙伴间全局与局部均趋于对齐，局部调整由颞顶/前颞区域支持并经左额下回实现自我更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效言语交流要求双方通过精确措辞对齐交际意图，但在动态、开放式交流中这一对齐如何实现尚不清楚。作者希望厘清语境约束与伙伴敏感调整在交流中的计算分工，并为理解人类及人-AI交流提供框架。

Method: 将被试的词汇选择用预测性深度语言模型（DLM）刻画，并以统计-语用混合模型形式化"局部调整"，采用受主动推断启发的更新规则。30组被试完成动态开放式协作词汇生成任务，同时进行fNIRS超扫描；比较多种模型以解释词汇对齐，并分析神经相关物，另在独立捷克语数据集上验证泛化性。

Result: 双方词汇选择趋于收敛，混合模型（语境选择先验+交互特异局部调整）解释力最佳。该过程形成由全局语义搜索区域与局部选择优先级构成的动态假设景观：个体内部全局语义搜索的假设空间逐渐收窄，而局部备择项间不确定性增大；伙伴间全局与局部均趋对齐，其中局部选择优先级的对齐最能预测随后的词汇对齐。局部调整在精确言语交流中强于控制任务。神经上，颞顶与前颞区域分别支持伙伴相关推断与语义预测误差，思考期活动预测说话期左额下回活动；自身假设景观由左顶下-缘上回表征。

Conclusion: 开放式交流中的对齐并非简单降低不确定性，而是由统计与语用两类互补计算共同涌现，可解释人类及人-AI交流中的对齐机制。

Abstract: Effective verbal communication requires individuals to align their communicative intentions through the precise use of utterances, yet how this is achieved in dynamic, open-ended communication remains unclear. Combining predictive deep language models (DLMs) with cognitive computational modeling, we tested a computational division of labor between context-sensitive global constraint on the utterance space and partner-sensitive local adjustment of selection priorities. We formalized local adjustment using an active inference inspired updating rule within a statistical-pragmatic hybrid model. Thirty dyads performed a dynamic, open-ended collaborative word-generation task during functional near infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) hyperscanning. Interlocutors converged in their lexical choices, and lexical alignment was best explained by a hybrid model integrating contextual selection priors with interaction-specific local adjustment among the models tested. This hybrid process formed a dynamic hypothesis landscape comprising a global semantic search region and local selection priorities. Critically, interaction did not simply reduce uncertainty: within individuals, the hypothesis space of global semantic search progressively narrowed while local uncertainty among plausible alternatives increased; between partners, both global hypothesis space and local selection became increasingly aligned, with alignment of local selection priorities most strongly predicting subsequent lexical alignment. The contribution of local adjustment was stronger during precise verbal communication than in the control tasks. Neural analyses further identified correlates of the local adjustment process: temporoparietal and anterior temporal regions supported partner-related inference and semantic prediction error, respectively, and their activity during thinking predicted subsequent inferior frontal activity associated with self-updating during speaking. The participant's own hypothesis landscape was also represented in the left inferior parietal-supramarginal cortex. The main findings generalized across language models and an independent Czech dataset. Together, these findings reveal that alignment in open-ended communication emerges from complementary statistical and pragmatic computations, providing a framework for understanding human and human-AI communication.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 威胁学习对生存至关重要，其缺陷是多种神经精神疾病的基础。人脑成像提示前岛叶皮层（AIC）是威胁学习的关键神经相关物，但其机制性作用尚不明确。

Method: 采用多模态系统神经科学手段，结合环路特异性操作，解析AIC兴奋性神经元（尤其是投射至基底外侧杏仁核的第5层锥体神经元）在不同威胁学习范式中的功能。

Result: AIC第5层→基底外侧杏仁核投射神经元是高阶威胁学习/记忆所必需：当预测线索与厌恶事件存在时间间隔，或预测信息嵌入弥散性情境时被特异性招募；而对基本的、时间毗连的威胁学习并非必需。此外，AIC虽促进记忆形成，却在提取阶段以情境依赖方式限制恐惧表达水平，该双向调控由不同AIC神经元集群介导。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一条对高阶威胁学习/记忆进行双向调控的新型环路机制，明确了AIC及其向基底外侧杏仁核投射的特异性贡献。

Abstract: Learning about potential threats is indispensable for survival, and its deficits underlie numerous neuropsychiatric disorders. Human brain imaging suggests the anterior insular cortex (AIC) as a key neural correlate of threat learning, yet its mechanistic role remains unclear. Leveraging multimodal systems neuroscience approaches, we demonstrate that AIC excitatory neurons, specifically layer 5 pyramidal neurons projecting to the basolateral amygdala, are essential for higher-order threat learning/memory. They are specifically required when a predictor is temporally separated from an aversive event or when the predictive information is embedded within a diffuse context. In contrast, the AIC is dispensable for elementary, temporally contiguous threat learning. Surprisingly, while the AIC promotes memory formation, it also constrains the level of fear expression during retrieval in a context-dependent manner. This bidirectional control is mediated by the recruitment of distinct AIC neuronal ensembles. Together, we identify a novel circuit mechanism for bidirectional regulation of higher-order threat learning/memory.

### [19] [Disrupting the Right Inferior Frontal Gyrus Increases Social Brain Activity During Emotion Recognition](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.08.757563v1)
*Kucuk, E.,Gofton, H.,Smith, C.,Kaiser, D.,Pitcher, D.*

TL;DR: 本研究结合经颅磁刺激(TMS)与功能磁共振成像(fMRI),发现对右侧额下回(IFG)的短暂干扰会增强社会脑网络远端节点(双侧颞极、楔前叶、内侧前额叶)的神经活动,表明IFG的自上而下控制机制受损会改变整个社会脑网络的神经加工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经影像学研究已确认支持情绪识别与社会认知的专门化社会脑网络,神经心理学患者研究显示该网络中的自上而下控制区——右侧额下回(IFG)受损会损害面部表情识别。然而,对右侧IFG的短暂干扰如何影响更广泛的社会脑网络尚不明确,本文旨在探究这一问题。

Result: 对IFG施加TMS增加了社会脑网络中多个区域的神经活动,包括双侧颞极、楔前叶和内侧前额叶皮层(mPFC)。

Abstract: Neuroimaging studies have identified a specialised social brain network that supports emotion recognition and social cognition. This network performs a range of functions that include facial expression recognition and inferring the mental states of others. Studies of neuropsychological patients report that damage to the right inferior frontal gyrus (IFG), the top-down control area in this network, impairs facial expression recognition. To investigate how transient disruption of the right IFG affects the wider social brain we combined transcranial magnetic stimulation (TMS) with functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI). Participants (n=20) were scanned across two sessions while performing a delayed match-to-sample emotion recognition task in which happy or fearful expressions were preceded by a congruent or incongruent word cue. Prior to each scan, thetaburst TMS was delivered over the right IFG or over the vertex control site (session order was balanced across participants). A group whole-brain analysis revealed that TMS to the IFG increased neural activity in multiple regions of the social brain network. These included the bilateral temporal pole, precuneus and medial prefrontal cortex (mPFC). This pattern of results shows that the disruption of top-down control mechanisms localised in the IFG alters the neural processing in remote nodes of the social brain network. Our findings are discussed within the wider context of how we recognise and understand the emotions of other people.

</details>


### [20] [Autonomic neurons are intrinsically hyperexcitable in a Friedreich ataxia iPSC model](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.06.757136v1)
*(Max), S. L.,Lees, J.,Zhang, H.,Li, L.,Dottori, M.,Pebay, A.,Corben, L.,Delatycki, M.,Wilcox, S.,Pullin, J.,Choi, J.,McCarthy, D.,Napierala, J.,Napierala, M.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRDA是由FXN基因GAA重复扩增导致frataxin缺乏引起的多系统遗传病，临床和尸检证据提示自主神经功能障碍（静息心率升高、血浆儿茶酚胺升高、心血管传导系统及神经节退变），但因缺乏人类细胞模型，无法判断这些异常是否为FRDA自主神经元的内在（细胞自主性）缺陷。

Result: FRDA自律神经元表达经典自主谱系标志物并保留FXN mRNA及frataxin蛋白的降低；电生理显示显著过度兴奋，动作电位数目约增加5倍、峰间隔缩短、爆发延长；同时出现细胞死亡增加、线粒体ROS升高及线粒体形态由网络状向管状转变；RNA测序显示细胞外基质相关程序丢失、应激反应通路激活；MEG3（母源印记长非编码RNA）为最显著上调基因，在三对同基因细胞系中一致上调；14q32 DLK1-DIO3位点的母源表达非编码转录本协同去抑制，且该改变特异于14q32，其他印记域未见协同变化。

Conclusion: frataxin缺乏可细胞自主性地导致人类自律神经元过度兴奋并伴随线粒体功能障碍，提示其为FRDA交感张力升高的细胞学基础，并为该病的自主神经症状及潜在治疗靶点（如14q32印记位点调控）提供依据。

Abstract: Friedreich ataxia (FRDA) is an inherited multisystem disorder caused by a GAA repeat expansion in the FXN gene and consequent frataxin deficiency, causing progressive neurodegeneration and heart disease. Clinical studies have documented autonomic dysfunction in FRDA, including elevated resting heart rate, raised plasma catecholamines and a high burden of urinary, bowel and sexual symptoms. Consistent with this, autopsy studies show degeneration of the cardiac conduction system and its associated ganglia in FRDA. Whether these features are intrinsic to FRDA autonomic neurons has been untestable without a human cellular model. Here, a human model of FRDA autonomic neurons was established by differentiating induced pluripotent stem cells (iPSCs) from individuals with FRDA and from their CRISPR/Cas9-corrected isogenic controls. FRDA autonomic neurons expressed canonical autonomic lineage markers and retained reduced FXN mRNA and frataxin protein levels. Electrophysiological analysis revealed pronounced hyperexcitability, with an approximately five-fold increase in spike number, shortened interspike intervals, and longer bursts. FRDA autonomic neurons also exhibited increased cell death, elevated mitochondrial reactive oxygen species, and a shift from networked to tubular mitochondrial morphology. Bulk RNA sequencing of all three isogenic pairs revealed loss of extracellular matrix programs and activation of stress response pathways. The top differentially expressed gene was MEG3, a maternally imprinted long noncoding RNA, which was consistently upregulated across all three isogenic pairs. MEG3 sits within the imprinted 14q32 DLK1-DIO3 locus, whose maternally expressed noncoding transcripts were co-ordinately de-repressed in FRDA autonomic neurons. Competitive gene-set testing of 43 imprinted genes across 20 domains showed this to be specific to 14q32: no other imprinted domain was co-ordinately altered. Together, these findings establish hyperexcitability of autonomic neurons as a cell autonomous consequence of frataxin deficiency, accompanied by mitochondrial dysfunction. This provides a cellular basis for the increased sympathetic tone observed in clinical studies.

</details>


### [21] [Serotonin2A receptors in the medial prefrontal cortex do not drive DOI-induced head-twitch responses in rodents](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.05.756681v1)
*Vuruputuri, R. M.,Reghunath, V.,Mukherjee, S.,Bajre, G.,Chaudhari, P. R.,Gahrotra, V.,Vaidya, V. A.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究探讨致幻剂DOI诱导的头部抽动反应（HTR）是否依赖内侧前额叶皮层（mPFC）的5-HT2A受体，结果表明该行为具有物种与性别特异性及5-HT2AR依赖性，但mPFC中的5-HT2AR并不参与HTR与耳挠反应的产生。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HTR是啮齿类模型中5-HT2AR依赖的致幻剂行为学指标。尽管皮层5-HT2AR被认为参与HTR，但具体皮层环路（尤其是mPFC）的贡献尚不明确，且相关报道较少。本研究旨在明确mPFC中5-HT2AR在DOI诱导的HTR和耳挠反应中的作用。

Method: 在雄性和雌性大鼠与小鼠中给予致幻剂DOI，观察HTR、耳挠反应（ESR）及挖洞、理毛行为，并比较5-HT2AR敲除（KO）与野生型小鼠的反应。采用药理学（mPFC局部微量注射DOI、局部给予选择性5-HT2AR拮抗剂MDL100907）和遗传学策略（在mPFC CaMKII阳性兴奋性神经元中病毒恢复5-HT2AR表达，以及条件性敲除5-HT2AR），评估mPFC 5-HT2AR对DOI诱导行为的调控作用。

Result: DOI在雌雄大鼠和小鼠中均诱导HTR，性别差异仅见于小鼠；DOI还增加ESR，并仅在小鼠中减少挖洞和理毛行为。DOI诱导的HTR和ESR在5-HT2AR KO小鼠中消失，但挖洞和理毛减少在野生型和KO小鼠中均出现。mPFC微量注射DOI不能诱导大鼠HTR；mPFC局部给予MDL100907不影响系统性DOI引起的HTR增加；在mPFC CaMKII阳性兴奋性神经元中恢复5-HT2AR表达不足以在KO小鼠中恢复DOI诱导的HTR或ESR；mPFC CaMKII阳性兴奋性神经元中条件性敲除5-HT2AR也不改变系统性DOI诱导的HTR和ESR。

Conclusion: DOI的行为效应具有物种和性别特异性，其诱导的HTR和ESR强烈依赖5-HT2AR；但mPFC中的5-HT2AR似乎不参与DOI引起的HTR和ESR增强，提示其他脑区或环路可能介导这些行为。

Abstract: Background The head-twitch response (HTR) is a serotonin2A receptor (5-HT2AR) dependent behavioral correlate of serotonergic psychedelic action in rodent models. Although cortical 5-HT2ARs have been implicated in HTRs, the contribution of specific cortical circuits to this behavior remains poorly defined, with a few reports implicating the medial prefrontal cortex (mPFC) in the generation of HTRs. Results The serotonergic psychedelic, 2,5-dimethoxy-4-iodoamphetamine (DOI), induced head-twitch responses (HTRs) in male and female rats and mice, with sex differences noted only in mice. DOI also increased ear-scratch responses (ESRs), and reduced digging and grooming behaviors only in mice. While the DOI-evoked HTRs and ESRs were absent in 5-HT2AR knock-out (5-HT2AR KO) mice, reduced digging and grooming occurred in both wild-type and 5-HT2AR KO mice. We assessed the role of 5-HT2ARs in the mPFC in regulating DOI-induced HTRs using pharmacological and genetic approaches. Targeted DOI microinfusion into the mPFC did not induce HTRs in rats, in contrast to the robust increase in HTRs noted following systemic DOI administration. Local delivery of the selective 5-HT2AR antagonist, MDL100907, into the mPFC also had no effect on the systemic DOI-evoked increase in HTRs in rats. Experiments capitalizing on genetic strategies to perturb 5-HT2AR expression in the mPFC, indicated that viral restoration of 5-HT2AR expression in mPFC CaMKII-positive excitatory neurons was not sufficient to reinstate DOI-evoked HTRs or ESRs in 5-HT2AR KO mice. Further, conditional 5-HT2AR deletion in CaMKII-positive excitatory neurons in the mPFC did not alter systemic DOI-induced HTRs and ESRs in mice. Conclusions These findings indicate that the serotonergic psychedelic DOI induces species- and sex-specific behavioral effects in rodent models, with a robust 5-HT2AR dependence noted for DOI-evoked HTRs and ESRs. Further, we show that 5-HT2ARs in the mPFC do not appear to contribute to the enhanced HTRs and ESRs elicited by DOI.

</details>

Abstract: Transient prenatal hypoxia is associated with long-term neurodevelopmental impairment without overt structural brain injury, suggesting early regulatory disruptions precede detectable pathology. Whether these disruptions represent transient stress responses or redirect developmental programs remains unknown. Here, we combined single-nucleus RNA sequencing and chromatin accessibility profiling in the late-gestation mouse cortex immediately after transient hypoxia, capturing responses across more than 140,000 nuclei. Hypoxia induced a largely conserved transcriptional stress response across cell types, whereas chromatin accessibility showed more extensive, cell type-specific disruption. Developing glutamatergic neurons showed the strongest disruption of chromatin transcription coupling, with hypoxia-responsive distal regulatory regions associated with genes governing synaptic organization and neuronal excitability. Consistent with these chromatin-associated programs, juvenile offspring displayed reduced dendritic spine density and increased intrinsic excitability in cortical neurons. These results support a model in which transient hypoxia co-opts the developing epigenome's capacity to encode future transcriptional states before persistent circuit dysfunction emerges.

Abstract: The ability to recover from consecutive failures is critical for adaptive behavior, yet the underlying neural mechanisms remain unclear. Targeted optogenetic activation of these frontal-sensory neurons disrupted cycles of attentional performance failure and reinstated behavioral performance in a mouse model of neuropsychiatric syndrome. ### [24] [Understanding neural circuit principles for representation learning through joint-embedding predictive architectures](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.1101/2025.11.25.690220v3)
*Mohammadi, A. G.,Halvagal, M. S.,Zenke, F.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该论文提出循环预测学习（RPL），一种受自监督学习启发的循环联合嵌入预测架构，将预测重点从输入空间转移到表征空间，仅通过无监督的连续感觉流学习物体身份与动态的抽象表征，并预测未来运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物生存需要在纠缠的感觉输入中同时提取物体身份与动态信息。经典预测编码理论虽捕捉了皮层计算的若干关键特征，但侧重在输入空间忠实预测感觉信号，未明确解释抽象的、解纠缠的物体及其动态表征如何仅通过经验自发涌现。

Method: 提出循环预测学习（RPL），一种基于自监督思想的循环联合嵌入预测架构，不依赖外部监督或标注数据。它在表征空间进行预测，学习物体身份与动态的抽象表征并预测未来物体运动；其中不变物体身份可由局部学习规则习得，而捕捉物体动态则需要能显式生成并最小化内部预测误差信号的回路；并进一步给出RPL的层级版本并讨论其与皮层微回路的可能映射。

Result: 仅局部学习规则即可学习不变的物体身份，但捕捉物体动态需显式生成并最小化内部预测误差信号的回路。模型学到的序列表征类似人类初级视觉皮层中观察到的后继式（successor-like）表征，并发展出与猕猴前额叶皮层报道相当的抽象序列表征；同时验证了RPL的层级版本。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了以回路为中心的理论框架，为大脑如何通过经验获得世界内部模型提供了新视角。

Abstract: Whether tracking prey or evading a predator, an animal's survival depends on both identifying objects in the environment and anticipating how they will move. To guide behavior, the brain must therefore extract information about object identities and their dynamics from entangled sensory inputs. How it accomplishes this feat remains an open question. Classical predictive coding theories propose that this ability arises by comparing predicted sensory signals with actual inputs and reducing the associated prediction errors. While such models capture important aspects of cortical computation, they typically focus on faithfully predicting sensory input and do not explicitly address how abstract, untangled representations of objects and their dynamics emerge solely through experience. Here, we develop a theory of representation learning in neural circuits that shifts the focus from prediction in the input space to prediction in representation space, without relying on external supervision or labeled data. Specifically, we introduce recurrent predictive learning (RPL), a recurrent joint-embedding predictive architecture inspired by self-supervised learning, that learns abstract representations of object identity and their dynamics and predicts future object motion from continuous sensory streams. While local learning rules alone can learn invariant object identity, we find that capturing object dynamics requires a circuit that explicitly generates and minimizes internal prediction-error signals. Crucially, the model learns sequence representations that resemble successor-like representations observed in the human primary visual cortex. The model also develops abstract sequence representations comparable to those reported in the macaque prefrontal cortex. Finally, we demonstrate a hierarchical version of RPL and discuss possible mappings onto cortical microcircuits. Our work establishes a circuit-centric theory framework that provides new perspectives on how the brain may acquire an internal model of the world through experience.

</details>

*Santana Agreda, Y. C.,Wright, K. M.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哺乳动物神经系统发育中，多能祖细胞可分化为数百种神经元亚型，其终末分化受转录因子调控。作者希望揭示调控神经元亚型终末身份获得与维持的具体转录因子机制，并选取小鼠视网膜SAC这一唯一胆碱能无长突细胞亚型作为模型。

Abstract: During mammalian nervous system development, multipotent progenitor cells differentiate into hundreds of distinct neuronal subtypes. Neuronal subtype terminal differentiation is regulated by transcription factors that directly control the acquisition and maintenance of terminal identity. Using the mouse retina as a model system, we show that the transcription factor ISL1 is required for the terminal differentiation and maintenance of starburst amacrine cells (SACs), the sole cholinergic neuron subtype in the retina. We conditionally deleted Isl1 selectively from amacrine and horizontal cell precursors, allowing us to assess its cell-autonomous role in SAC development without disrupting its critical function in bipolar cell and retinal ganglion cell development. We find that ISL1 is not required for initial SAC specification but is required for their terminal differentiation. Isl1-deficient SACs retain a GABAergic amacrine cell identity but fail to adopt many SAC-specific features including expression of genes required for cholinergic transmission. In the absence of ISL1, SACs fail to migrate properly, with both OFF- and ON- SACs localizing to the inner nuclear layer. Deletion of Isl1 from post-migratory SACs at later stages of development revealed that it is also required for maintaining SAC identity and proper dendrite stratification. Loss of Isl1 from SACs ultimately caused abnormal development of the direction selective circuit. Together, these findings establish that ISL1 is required for the induction and maintenance of SAC-specific identity from a generic amacrine state.

### [26] [Optimizing Intermittent Theta-Burst Stimulation: The Effects of Pulse Parameters on Corticospinal Excitability](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.10.05.756785v1)
*Proulx, B.,Hasani, S. A.,Frechette, A.-J.,Hamel, R.,Tremblay, S.,Cote, S.,Lepage, J.-F.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

Abstract: Objective. Intermittent theta burst stimulation (iTBS) is a repetitive transcranial magnetic stimulation (rTMS) protocol used in clinical settings to alleviate symptoms of psychiatric disorders. Although the pulse parameters currently used in iTBS have remained unchanged since the first treatments, recent controllable TMS devices allow for the personalization of pulse shape and duration, opening the possibility of testing the efficacy of other pulse parameters in modulating corticospinal excitability (CSE). Methods. In a repeated-measures design, changes in CSE were induced by the administration of iTBS over the left primary motor cortex under three different conditions: long bidirectional pulse (L-Bi), long quasi-unidirectional pulse (L-QU), and a control short quasi-unidirectional pulse (S-QU). We assessed TMS-induced motor evoked potentials before and up to 70 minutes after iTBS. We hypothesized that L-QU pulses would induce greater facilitation than other conditions. Results. A mixed linear model showed that all conditions induced increased MEPs compared to baseline. However, the L-Bi pulse led to a larger and more sustained MEP increase than both unidirectional pulses, while the S-QU pulse appeared less effective in modulating CSE. We observed large within- and between-subject variability in responsiveness to the iTBS protocols. Conclusion. These results suggest that L-Bi pulses in iTBS protocols are more potent than unidirectional pulses in inducing CSE changes in the cortex of healthy adults. The individual variability in response to iTBS and the potential clinical relevance of these findings are discussed.

</details>

### [27] [Algebraic Integer Quantization for Machine Learning Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12494)
*Anthony Kostal-Vazquez,Vassil Dimitrov,Branden Butler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出代数整数数值格式（AINF）作为高度可配置且精确的压缩计算框架，其核心是可无损归约为纯整数运算，从而实现无误差的乘累加；作者据此构建矩阵乘法引擎，并进一步提出面向大语言模型推理的有损压缩格式——代数整数量化（AIQ），在相同比特预算下取得与MX格式相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浮点与定点格式在数值表示上存在固有约束，无法针对具体用例的数值特征灵活选择表示形式；同时压缩计算中乘累加误差难以避免。作者希望利用代数整数的代数结构，构建一个既可精确归约为整数运算、又能按用例定制数值特性的计算与量化框架。

Method: 1) 提出代数整数数值格式（AINF），其关键性质是可直接归约为纯整数算术，支持无误差的乘累加运算；2) 基于该性质开发矩阵乘法引擎，在现代硬件加速器上与最高效的数值实现相比性能处于可预测的倍数范围内；3) 提出代数整数量化（AIQ），一种用于大语言模型推理的有损压缩格式，可利用代数整数的选择自由度匹配给定用例的数值特征；4) 实验采用二次代数整数（degree two, quadratic integers）。

Result: 在多样化的模型与基准测试上，于等效的每参数比特预算下，AIQ 取得了与业界标准 MX 格式具有竞争力的精度；所开发的矩阵乘法引擎性能与最优数值实现相比处于可预测因子范围内。

Conclusion: AINF 提供了高度可配置、精确的压缩计算框架，AIQ 在 LLM 推理中展现出与 MX 格式相当的精度。本文实验局限于二次代数整数；向高阶、尤其是多维代数整数的推广有望带来更大的计算优势，是未来有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: In this article, we propose algebraic integer number formats (AINF) as a framework for highly configurable and accurate compressed computation. A key property of AINF is its straightforward reduction to purely integer arithmetic, enabling error-free multiply-accumulate operations. We develop a matrix multiplication engine that exploits this property, obtaining performance within a predictable factor of the most efficient numerical counterparts on modern hardware accelerators. Another key advantage of AIQ is the freedom to select algebraic integers tailored to the numerical characteristics of a given use case, offering optimization opportunities that are fundamentally unavailable in floating-point or fixed-point formats. Motivated by these properties, we introduce algebraic integer quantization (AIQ), a lossy compression format for large language model (LLM) inference, and provide results demonstrating that AIQ achieves competitive accuracy compared to the industry-standard MX format across a diverse set of models and benchmarks at equivalent bit-per-parameter budgets. The experiments presented in this article employ algebraic integers of degree two (quadratic integers); generalizations to higher-order and, in particular, multidimensional algebraic integers promise even greater computational advantages and remain a promising direction for future work.

</details>


### [28] [Kairos: Toward a Byte-Level Foundation Model for Every Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12495)
*Fabien Furfaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kairos架构并仅做收敛性"体检"：在单机、1–15M参数、单句440字节文本上验证各组件能正常优化，明确不对泛化、多模态与模型对比作任何结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单机小模型缺乏架构试错空间，作者希望在投入大规模预训练前，先确认这套复杂组件组合是否至少能被优化，即"先问能否收敛，再问是否有用"。

Method: 架构集成：LiZAttention2（共享投影上的双向门控DeltaNet线性注意力+滑窗注意力）、块级Attention-Residual聚合、稀疏MoE前馈层、多尺度字节级编解码器，并以统一噪声去噪目标在文本、图像、音频、视频、lidar与控制共享的字节空间中训练；给出各组件代码派生算法（含跨会话循环状态记忆门）。实验为收敛性检验：单句440字节过拟合，1–15M参数，仅文本；编解码器与注意力选择用3个随机种子，其余7个因素单次扫描。同时给出200M总参/25M激活目标的分析性规模估计。

Result: 两种注意力变体（LiZAttention2与纯窗口注意力）在3000步时最低损失约10⁻³。600步后平均最低损失从0.018（patch编解码器）到0.354（深度卷积编解码器），三随机种子排序一致。共享注意力投影相对独立投影的表面优势随步数增加或数据多样性提升而消失，冻结投影时甚至反转，说明这是收敛速度效应而非容量效应。

Conclusion: 该实现确实能被优化，但结论仅限于收敛性，不涉及泛化、多模态训练或与现有模型对比，这些留给计划中的预训练。讨论中200M/25M目标的吞吐数字均为分析估算，未经实测。代码与Python包已开源。

Abstract: Small language models trained on a single local machine leave little room for architectural trial and error. Kairos combines bidirectional gated-DeltaNet linear attention and sliding-window attention over shared projections (LiZAttention2), block-wise Attention-Residual aggregation, a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) feed-forward layer and a multi-scale byte-level codec, trained with a uniform-noise denoising objective over one byte space shared by text, image, audio, video, lidar and control streams; this report asks whether the combination optimizes at all before asking whether it is useful. We give code-derived algorithms for each component, including a cross-session recurrent-state memory gate, then run convergence sanity checks: overfittinga single 440-byte sentence at 1--15M parameters, text only, with three seeds for codec and attention choices and single-run sweeps for seven further factors. The two attention variants tested at 3\,000 steps (LiZAttention2 and window attention alone) reach a minimum loss near $10^{-3}$. After 600 steps, the mean minimum loss ranges from 0.018 (patch codec) to 0.354 (depthwise convolutional codec) with an identical ranking on three seeds. An apparent advantage of shared over separate attention projections closes with more steps or more diverse data and reverses when the projections are frozen: it is a convergence-speed effect, not a capacity effect. These checks suggest that the implementation optimizes; they say nothing about generalization, multimodal training or comparison with existing models, which a planned pretraining run is meant to address. The Discussion sizes a 200M-total/25M-active target analytically; none of its throughput figures is measured. Code is available at https://github.com/fabienfrfr/Kairos . Python package at https://pypi.org/project/kairos-fm/ .

</details>


### [29] [An Input-Dependent Readout Phase for Resonant Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12496)
*Shivesh Rane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将 ResonatorLM 中固定的读出相位改为依赖当前 token 的函数，仅对"读"操作引入输入依赖，保持卷积精确性与参数效率（+0.2%），在 6M 字符级模型上将训练长度外推的困惑度从 12.81 降至 3.76。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定状态序列模型避免了注意力机制的二次开销，但面临设计权衡：输入依赖的状态转移带来内容相关的记忆，却牺牲了基于 FFT 的训练效率；固定转移保留了 O(T log T) 卷积路径，却对每个输入以相同方式读取记忆。作者希望在保持卷积精确、参数高效的前提下，为读操作引入输入依赖性。

Method: ResonatorLM 通过每个头固定的学习相位读取一池阻尼复振荡器。作者将读出相位改为当前 token 的函数，仅使读操作具备输入依赖性，卷积仍保持精确，仅增加 0.2% 参数。此外对未训练振荡器池进行分析，考察相位对经过时间的编码能力。

Result: 未训练池分析显示，相位确实编码了经过时间，但普通 token 在 2–8 步内便低于背景，且无论怎样选择阻尼，线性重构均无法超过约 16 个 token。在 WikiText-2 的 6M 参数字符级模型上，输入依赖相位在训练长度上与原模型相当，并在训练长度之外消除其失效：以 256 token 训练、1024 token 测试时，困惑度由 12.81 降至 3.76。

Conclusion: 失效可追溯至那些累积的历史超出训练所暴露范围的长记忆头。学习到的相位追随当前字符而非距离，因此并未改善检索性能。

Abstract: Sequence models with a fixed-size state avoid the quadratic cost of attention, but face a design choice. An input-dependent state transition gives content-dependent memory at the cost of FFT-based training, while a fixed transition keeps the O(T log T) convolutional path but reads memory the same way for every input. ResonatorLM, a recent model of the second kind, reads a bank of damped complex oscillators through a fixed learned phase per head. We make that readout phase a function of the current token, which adds input dependence to the read only, keeps the convolution exact, and costs 0.2 percent more parameters. An analysis of the untrained bank shows that phase does encode elapsed time, but an ordinary token falls below the background within 2-8 steps and no choice of damping extends linear reconstruction beyond about 16 tokens. In 6M-parameter character-level models on WikiText-2, the input-dependent phase matches the original at the training length and removes its failure beyond it: trained on 256 tokens and tested on 1024, perplexity falls from 12.81 to 3.76. We trace the failure to long-memory heads that accumulate more history than training exposed them to. The learned phase tracks the current character rather than distance and does not improve retrieval.

</details>


### [30] [CircuitTree: Composing Circuits into A Syllable Transcriber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12520)
*Jiankun Wei,Ewan Dunbar,Gerald Penn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究电路发现中的电路组合问题，提出密度缩放贪心组合算法并将其推广为CircuitTree，实现任意数量电路的高效组合。作者将HuBERT中39个音素电路组合为音节分类器，并以电路树层级管线实现音节转录，在共享99.5%组件的情况下达到64.8%准确率，接近66.0%的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路发现旨在将单体神经网络分解为可解释的模块化子网络，但如何将多个已发现的电路组合成单一模型仍具挑战；此前的简单并集组合方式存在局限，难以扩展到任意数量的电路。

Method: 提出贪心、密度缩放的电路组合算法，克服朴素并集组合的局限；随后将其推广至N个电路，设计了CircuitTree数据结构，基于Θ(N log N)的树形组合调度；并将多个CircuitTree串联为电路层级管线。

Result: 将HuBERT中为单个音素分类剪枝得到的电路组合成音节分类器，具备任一音素电路单独都不具备的能力；进一步串联CircuitTree构成层级管线，在开放音节转录任务上达到64.8%准确率，仅比不共享任何电路的66.0%上限低1.2个百分点，且共享了99.5%的电路组件。

Conclusion: 所提出的密度缩放贪心组合与CircuitTree方法能够高效、可扩展地组合大量电路，并在高组件共享率下保持接近独立电路上限的性能，验证了电路组合作为构建模块化可解释模型的可行路径。

Abstract: The goal of circuit discovery is to decompose a monolithic neural network into tiny modular subnetworks which are interpretable in the sense that each does an identifiable subtask. We develop a practical method for composing arbitrarily many circuits into a single model. We propose a greedy, density-scaled composition algorithm that overcomes limitations of previous attempts to compose circuits by naive union, then generalize it to $N$ circuits via CircuitTree, a new data structure built on a $Θ(N\log N)$ tree-compose schedule. We compose circuits pruned from HuBERT for classifying individual phonemes into a syllable classifier, a capability none of the 39 phoneme circuits has individually. Chaining CircuitTrees into a hierarchy-of-circuits pipeline, we transcribe open syllables at 64.8\% accuracy, within 1.2 points of a 66.0\% ceiling that shares no circuitry at all, with 99.5\% of circuit components shared.

</details>


### [31] [Behind-the-Meter Photovoltaic Visibility from Net Smart Meters: Deployment Regimes and Operational Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12527)
*Jack Zheng,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过四种部署场景的基准测试量化光伏分解模型误差对部署条件的依赖，发现部署场景比模型选择更能解释误差差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电运营商需要掌握表后光伏发电情况，但多数居民用户仅有净智能电表观测。深度学习的PV分解可恢复该可见性，然而其运行价值取决于模型是服务于已知用户、新用户、未来时段还是新区域，这一依赖关系此前缺乏系统量化。

Method: 提出四种部署场景（区分家庭迁移与时间外推），在Ausgrid智能电表数据上，并跨数据集适配到Pecan Street数据；采用固定训练协议，对代表性深度序列架构在场景匹配评估下进行基准测试，并考察目标域归一化微调与输入窗口长度的影响。

Result: 在48小时固定上下文长度下，最佳模型白天MAE从未来测试期中已见家庭的0.104 kW升至同一时段未见家庭的0.185 kW；固定训练契约下，部署场景对误差变异的解释力远大于模型选择。目标域归一化下，用5个目标区域家庭微调可将源模型迁移误差降低28%–36%。长输入窗口有助于已知用户外推，但对新用户的收益因模型而异。

Conclusion: 建议采用场景匹配的误差预算、在新区域模型适配中使用有限目标监督，以及按用例选择上下文长度，以支持能源数据分析实践。

Abstract: Distribution operators need visibility of behind-the-meter photovoltaic (PV) generation, yet most residential customers are observed only through net smart meters. Deep-learning-based PV disaggregation can restore this visibility, but its operational value depends on whether the model serves known customers, new customers, future periods, or new regions. We quantify this dependence using four deployment regimes that separate household transfer from temporal extrapolation, applied to Ausgrid smart-meter data with cross-dataset adaptation to the Pecan Street data. Using a fixed training protocol, we benchmark representative deep sequence architectures for PV disaggregation under regime-matched evaluation. At a fixed 48-hour context length, daylight mean absolute error (MAE) of the best model rises from 0.104~kW on seen households in the future test period to 0.185~kW on unseen households over the same period, and under a fixed training contract the deployment regime explains far more of the error variation than model choice. Under target-domain normalization, fine-tuning on five target-region households reduces source-model transfer error by 28\%--36\%. Long input windows help known-customer extrapolation, while their new-customer benefits vary by model. These findings suggest regime-matched error budgets, limited target supervision for model adaptation in new regions, and use-case-dependent context selection for energy data analytics.

</details>


### [32] [GlucoPRISM: Protocol-Supervised Pretraining for Cross-Cohort Glucose Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12528)
*Adinath Madhavrao Dukre,Abdullah Mohammad,Yifan Lu,Eran Segal,Ahmed Metwally,Imran Razzak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GlucoPRISM通过响应契约监督GlucoFM表征，将设备/测量成分解耦到已知坐标，部署时用固定投影丢弃Sensor带，跨队列迁移AUROC从68.75提升至73.57。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续血糖监测（CGM）基础模型将每天编码为单一向量，混合了"人是谁""当天发生了什么"和"传感器如何测量"三类信息，导致测量差异随表征迁移到新队列，损害跨队列泛化。作者希望预训练能把有害的测量成分放入已知坐标，从而在部署时仅靠固定投影丢弃，无需目标数据。

Method: 提出GlucoPRISM：用"响应契约"监督GlucoFM主干——表征被划分为64维Trait带（对天、设备均不变化）、48维State带（仅随天变化）、16维Sensor带（追踪设备）。监督信号来自在配对Dexcom与Libre记录上校准的合成传感器视图、不同天配对及血糖汇总目标，无需临床标签。部署时从冻结编码器中直接丢弃Sensor带。

Result: 在12个跨队列方向上，丢弃Sensor带使平均迁移AUROC从68.75升至73.57（GlucoFM为69.3）。增益分两部分：进入CGMacros（唯一用两种传感器记录的队列）过滤带来11–19点提升，且在生成器半数受试者重校准、另一半评分时依然成立；其余8个方向过滤中性，而编码器本身超出GlucoFM 3.5点。过滤后设备仍线性可解码，说明去除的是有害设备相关成分而非全部测量信息。保留带在14个公开任务–队列单元中的13个改善窗口AUROC（Holm校正p=0.031），并在9,303人外部队列的15个临床终点中的14个改善相关性。

Conclusion: 通过响应契约监督可将CGM表征中的设备/测量成分解耦到显式维度，部署时用固定投影即可移除有害迁移成分，既提升跨队列泛化又保留可用的测量信息，且在多种任务与外部大队列中一致有效。

Abstract: Continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) foundation models encode each day as one vector that mixes who the person is, what happened that day and how the sensor measured it, so measurement differences travel with the representation into new cohorts. We ask whether pretraining can place the measurement component that harms transfer in known coordinates, so that deployment drops them with a fixed projection and no target data. GlucoPRISM supervises a GlucoFM backbone with response contracts, rules for how each part of the representation should change when only the day or only the sensor changes: a 64-dimensional Trait band should change with neither, a 48-dimensional State band only across days, and a 16-dimensional Sensor band should track the device. Synthetic sensor views calibrated on paired Dexcom and Libre recordings, different-day pairs and glucose-summary targets supply this supervision without clinical labels. Across twelve cross-cohort directions, dropping the Sensor band from the same frozen encoder raises mean transfer AUROC from 68.75 to 73.57, against 69.3 for GlucoFM. The gain has two parts. Into CGMacros, the one target cohort recorded with two sensor types, filtering adds 11 to 19 points, and this holds when the generator is recalibrated on half of the participants and scored on the other half. On the other eight directions filtering is neutral and the encoder itself exceeds GlucoFM by 3.5 points. The device remains linearly decodable after filtering, so the filter removes a device-related component that harms transfer rather than all measurement information. The retained bands improve window AUROC on thirteen of fourteen public task--cohort cells (Holm-adjusted p=0.031) and correlation on fourteen of fifteen clinical endpoints in an external cohort of 9,303 participants.

</details>


### [33] [CacheScope: Exact-Compute Allocation for Training-Free Diffusion Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12530)
*Qinhan Lyu,Jusheng Zhang,Zhixing Zhang,Hao Liu,Zijie Liang,Jin Jiang,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CacheScope，一种无需训练的扩散 Transformer 推理加速方法：通过将探针输出变化按输入位移归一化得到"方向性增益"信号，在线识别高风险缓存复用步并选择性回退到完整去噪计算，在多个模型上实现最高 3.57 倍加速并将中位 LPIPS 降低最多 70.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练特征复用方法主要致力于改进被跳过步的特征重建质量，但作者发现：一旦采用稳定的 一阶重建器，继续提升重建复杂度带来的收益有限，而精确计算步的"放置位置"对保真度影响更大。此外，绝对浅层探针变化同时受输入轨迹位移与网络响应强度两方面影响，是混淆信号，不能直接反映缓存风险。

Method: 对浅层探针输出变化以相应的输入位移进行归一化，得到"每单位轨迹移动的方向性增益"这一解耦信号；基于该信号提出 CacheScope，在推理过程中在线识别高风险复用步，并对这些步选择性地回退到完整去噪计算，同时保持底层缓存重建器不变。方法无需额外训练、梯度、辅助网络或针对提示词的离线调度优化。

Result: 在 FLUX.1-dev、Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B 和 Open-Sora 1.2 上，CacheScope 相比最强的基于探针的基线方法，实现最高 3.57 倍加速，并将中位 LPIPS 最多降低 70.1%。

Conclusion: 缓存复用的关键瓶颈不在重建器复杂度，而在精确计算步的调度位置；通过对探针信号进行输入位移归一化可解耦混淆因素、更可靠地度量缓存风险，从而以零额外训练代价显著提升扩散 Transformer 推理加速下的生成保真度。

Abstract: Training-free feature reuse accelerates diffusion transformer inference by replacing full denoising computation at selected steps with cached approximations. Existing methods mainly improve the reconstruction of skipped features, but we find that once a stable first-order reconstructor is used, increasing reconstruction complexity brings limited gains, whereas the placement of exact-computation steps has a much larger effect on fidelity. More importantly, absolute shallow-probe variation is jointly affected by input-trajectory displacement and network response strength, and therefore does not directly reflect cache risk. We address this confounding by normalizing probe-output variation with the corresponding input displacement, yielding a directional gain per unit trajectory movement. Based on this signal, we propose CacheScope, which identifies high-risk reuse steps online and selectively falls back to full denoising computation while leaving the underlying cache reconstructor unchanged. CacheScope requires no additional training, gradients, auxiliary networks, or prompt-specific offline schedule optimization. Across FLUX.1-dev, Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B, and Open-Sora 1.2, CacheScope achieves up to 3.57x acceleration and reduces median LPIPS by up to 70.1% over the strongest probe-based baseline.

</details>


### [34] [Market Impact Estimation via Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12537)
*Dennis Thumm,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概念验证性的"因果市场模拟器"——一个连续时间因果先验数据拟合网络（PFN），在构造性含混淆的合成市场先验上训练，用于估计市场冲击这一因果量，并在模拟盘与真实数据上验证其零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交易者执行大单时真正关心的是反事实问题："若我没有交易，价格会是多少？"因此市场冲击本质是因果量。然而经典模型（Almgren–Chriss、propagator）将价格对订单流做回归，当知情流同时通过信息渠道和交易渠道推动价格时会产生偏误。现有方法缺乏对混淆的处理与因果识别框架。

Method: 构建连续时间因果先验数据拟合网络（PFN），在合成市场先验上训练：先验中隐藏的知情流按构造混淆订单流—价格关系，且混淆强度是逐 episode 精确可控的旋钮。方法上对比三类模型：朴素估计器、PFN、以及去掉干预输入的"do-消融孪生模型"；并引入循环状态空间变体以改进冲击的时间形状。迁移验证包括 ABIDES 限价订单簿上的 metaorder 零样本测试、BTC/ETH/SOL 永续合约约 2300 个流突发及纳斯达克股票约 3800 个样本的干预响应复现，以及 split-conformal 重校准。

Result: 朴素估计器偏误有闭式解并获实证确认（最高高估 68%），PFN 在高混淆下贴合无偏真参数 oracle；do-消融孪生模型坍缩至窗口前均值。零样本下模型对 ABIDES 中的 metaorder 以真实尺度响应（+0.38 个 onset 前标准差，对照种子重放反事实），并在约 2300 个加密永续与约 3800 个纳斯达克流突发上复现干预响应，虽非因果迁移的证明但与之一致。分布外响应优雅退化，split-conformal 重校准可修复区间标定。迁移关键在于将 metaorder 编码为"新增流"而非钳制价格水平，而非增加训练量。循环状态空间变体在真实数据上把与实测冲击的相关性从 +0.68 提升至 +0.92–0.96，但其在先验上的去混淆能力较弱，源于训练配方而非循环结构本身。

Conclusion: 市场冲击应作为因果量建模，连续时间因果 PFN 提供了一条可行路径：合成混淆先验可实现精确的因果识别与去混淆、并支持向真实市场的零样本迁移；编码方式（新增流 vs 钳制价格）比训练规模更决定迁移成败。循环结构改善冲击时间形状，而去混淆强度取决于训练配方。整体为概念验证，跨市场证据"与因果迁移一致"但尚非证明。

Abstract: When a trader executes a large order, the relevant question is not \emph{what will the price be?} but \emph{what would the price have been had I not traded?} Market impact is thus a causal quantity, yet classical models (Almgren--Chriss, propagators) regress price on flow, which is biased whenever informed flow moves prices through information as well as trading. We present a proof-of-concept \emph{Causal Market Simulator}: a continuous-time causal prior-data fitted network (PFN) trained on a synthetic market prior in which a hidden informational flow confounds the flow--price relation by construction, with confounding strength an exact per-episode knob. On this prior, the naive estimator's bias follows a closed form we confirm empirically (up to 68\% overestimation), while the PFN tracks the unbiased true-parameter oracle at high confounding. Its \emph{do-ablated twin}, the same model with the intervention inputs removed, collapses onto the pre-window mean. Zero-shot, the model responds at the ground-truth scale to metaorders in an ABIDES limit-order book ($+0.38$ pre-onset standard deviations against seed-replay counterfactuals), and its intervention response replicates on about 2,300 detected flow bursts in BTC/ETH/SOL perpetuals and 3,800 on Nasdaq equities, evidence consistent with, though not proof of, causal transfer. Off-prior the response degrades gracefully, and split-conformal recalibration repairs interval calibration. Transfer hinges on encoding the metaorder as added flow rather than a clamped price level, not on more training. A recurrent state-space variant closes the remaining shape gap: its impact accrues over execution duration where the transformer's stays flat, raising the correlation with measured metaorder impact from $+0.68$ to between $+0.92$ and $+0.96$, at the cost of weaker on-prior deconfounding that traces to the training recipe rather than to recurrence.

</details>

*Itai Zilberstein,Ioannis Anagnostides,Arman Kilic,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究结果依赖型截断下的学习问题，连接截断学习与生存分析两个方向，证明在基线风险已知时可用一阶优化高效学习Cox回归，而联合学习基线风险时负对数似然存在严格次优局部极小值，并通过实验验证截断建模能降低预测偏差并大幅提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习模型通常假设训练数据能代表未来预测任务，但实际数据集往往违反该假设。论文聚焦结果依赖型截断，即某观测是否进入训练数据取决于其标签。现有截断生存分析方法未刻画优化景观，仅依赖EM等迭代过程，缺乏理论保障，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 论文将截断下的高效学习与生存分析两个相对独立的研究领域相连接。针对截断且右删失的数据，作者分析Cox回归的优化性质：在基线风险已知的情形下证明可借助一阶优化高效学习；在基线风险需联合学习的情形下，构造可识别且正确设定的模型，并分析其负对数似然的局部极小值结构。实验在四个生存数据集上进行，并扩展至神经网络。

Result: 理论上，作者证明基线风险已知时，尽管问题非凸，Cox回归仍可用一阶优化高效学习；而联合学习基线风险时，存在可识别且正确设定的模型，其负对数似然含有严格次优局部极小值。实验中，四个生存数据集表明考虑截断可降低朴素Cox回归带来的预测偏差，并适用于神经网络，同时相比标准EM方法获得数量级的速度提升。

Conclusion: 论文刻画了截断生存分析中Cox回归的优化景观，明确了何时可高效优化、何时存在内在困难，并将截断建模的有效性扩展至神经网络，显著优于传统EM方法。

Abstract: Modern machine learning models are typically trained on data assumed to be representative of the predictive tasks the model will face in the future, but practical datasets violate this assumption. We focus on outcome-dependent truncation in which an observation appears in the training data depending on its label. Our work connects two largely distinct literatures: efficient learning under truncation and survival analysis. Existing methods for truncated survival analysis do not characterize the optimization landscape and instead rely on iterative procedures such as expectation maximization. We address this gap and prove that Cox regression can be efficiently learned under truncated and right-censored data using first-order optimization when the baseline hazard is known (despite nonconvexity). In contrast, when the baseline hazard is learned jointly, we construct identifiable and correctly specified models whose negative log-likelihood contains strict suboptimal local minima. On experiments with four survival datasets, we show that accounting for truncation reduces the prediction bias induced by naive Cox regression and extends to neural networks. We also show orders of magnitude speedups compared to standard expectation maximization.

</details>
